At this boundary HGA training is already marginally faster (217.75 vs. 207.02 tokens/s), and the HGA-to-dense throughput ratio improves from 1K to 2K; because HGA keeps the attended historical set per token approximately constant while dense work per token grows, we expect this lead to widen as context grows. Dense attention is used for the main quality and retrieval comparisons so that they measure the learned weights and remain compatible with standard generation frameworks. HGA can also be used for retrieval and generation; an optimized production-grade serving implementation is under development.

</details>


### [207] [Concept-Guided Spatial Regularization for World Models in Atari Pong](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15142)
*Yukuan Lu,Zaishuo Xia,Weyl Lu,Yubei Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在Atari Pong中评估五种视觉世界模型代理，发现闭环回放和零样本MBRL中存在严重失效，提出概念引导空间正则化(CGSReg)可改善部分模型性能，但未解决所有瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型通常作为基于模型的强化学习(MBRL)系统组件评估，而鲜有单独研究世界模型本身，本文旨在隔离评估世界模型以揭示其局限性。

Method: 复现五种代理(DreamerV3, DIAMOND, TWISTER, Simulus, STORM)训练流程并冻结世界模型，采用闭环回放诊断(独立策略与冻结模型交互检查轨迹)和像素空间零样本MBRL(在冻结模型内训练新策略再在真实环境评估)评估；提出概念引导空间正则化(CGSReg)，即对分割概念区域(如球)施加辅助像素重建损失。

Result: 所有五种模型的闭环回放均出现球消失、球运动错误、球拍交互无效等失效；零样本MBRL中新策略显著弱于原MBRL管道，DreamerV3平均回报从-5.5降至-20.9(接近最小-21)；CGSReg在DreamerV3, DIAMOND, TWISTER上改善闭环回放和零样本MBRL，但对其余模型和指标效果不一。

Conclusion: 任务关键概念(如球)建模不足可能导致失效；CGSReg虽能缓解部分问题，但无法解决所有世界模型瓶颈。

Abstract: World models are usually evaluated as components of model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) systems, while the world models themselves are rarely studied in isolation.
  We examine five representative visual world-model agents in Atari Pong: DreamerV3, DIAMOND, TWISTER, Simulus, and STORM. After reproducing their training pipelines and matching the reported agent performance, we freeze the learned world models and evaluate them with a closed-loop rollout diagnostic: a policy trained separately from the corresponding MBRL agent interacts with each frozen model, and the generated video trajectories are inspected for visual and dynamical errors. Across all five models, the rollouts contain clear failures, including ball disappearance, incorrect ball motion, and invalid ball-paddle interactions.
  Beyond visual trajectories, we further evaluate them with pixel-space zero-shot MBRL, where a new policy is trained entirely inside a frozen world model and then evaluated in the real environment. Across all five models, the resulting policies substantially underperform those produced by the corresponding original MBRL training pipelines. The gap is particularly large for DreamerV3, whose mean return drops from -5.5 to -20.9, near the minimum Pong return of -21.
  We hypothesize that insufficient modeling of task-critical concepts, such as the ball in Pong, may contribute to these failures. We therefore propose Concept-Guided Spatial Regularization (CGSReg), an auxiliary pixel reconstruction loss applied to segmented concept regions. Experiments show that CGSReg improves both closed-loop rollouts and pixel-space zero-shot MBRL in DreamerV3, DIAMOND, and TWISTER. Its effects vary across the remaining models and evaluation metrics, indicating that CGSReg alone does not address all world-model bottlenecks.

</details>


### [208] [The Industrialization of Research ; On AI-Driven Science and Its Consequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15164)
*Emmanuel Jeannot*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨人工智能对科学研究的变革影响，指出研究从“手工作坊模式”向“流水线模式”转型，提出七大核心问题：科学能力代际传承侵蚀、AI生成理论的不透明性、同行评审在机器生成输出冲击下的崩溃、AI范式转换发现能力未被证实、科学议程被政治和工业势力捕获、闭环流水线中系统性误差的累积、全球研究社区结构性分裂为不可通约的层级。这些担忧并非反对AI驱动科学，而是负责任地追求其潜力的前提条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在系统梳理AI自主参与研究周期所引发的深层结构性风险，超越单一项目（如美国能源部“起源任务”）的具体实践，为AI驱动科学的负责任发展提供分析框架。

Method: 采用哲学与社会学视角的概念分析法，通过对比“手工模式”与“流水线模式”，归纳并论证七个跨学科的根本性问题，结合当前大型AI科研计划的实例进行批判性审视。

Result: 识别出七大系统性风险：1) 代际隐性知识传承断裂；2) AI理论生成机制的黑箱化导致可解释性危机；3) 机器海量输出冲垮传统同行评审；4) AI在范式转换性发现上的能力仍属推测；5) 科研议程易被权力资本绑架；6) 闭环自动化放大系统性偏差；7) 全球科研资源与能力的结构性分层固化。

Conclusion: AI驱动科学的巨大潜力毋庸置疑，但必须在正视并应对上述七大条件的前提下推进，否则将导致科学理性的异化与不平等的固化；负责任的AI科研需要建立新的治理机制、评价体系与能力传承路径。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is transforming scientific research -- not merely as a more powerful instrument, but as an autonomous participant in the research cycle itself. This transition constitutes, in the most precise sense of the term, the industrialization of research: a shift from a craft model, in which knowledge, method, and judgment are embedded in the researcher, to a pipeline model, in which these steps are decomposed, automated, and supervised. The US Department of Energy's Genesis Mission is the most ambitious current instantiation of this shift, but the fundamental questions it raises extend far beyond any single program. This essay examines seven such questions: the erosion of the intergenerational transmission of scientific competence; the growing opacity of AI-generated theories; the collapse of peer evaluation under a flood of machine-generated output; the unproven capacity of AI for paradigm-shifting discovery; the capture of the scientific agenda by political and industrial actors; the compounding of systematic errors in closed-loop pipelines; and the structural bifurcation of the global research community into incommensurable tiers. These concerns do not constitute an argument against AI-driven science -- whose demonstrated potential is real and significant. They constitute the conditions under which that potential can be responsibly pursued.

</details>


### [209] [MedFailBench: A Clinician-Built Open-Source Benchmark for Medical AI Safety Boundary Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15166)
*Goktug Ozkan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MedFailBench是一个临床医生构建的合成基准和故障图谱，用于按严重程度（1-5级）和安全门类型（六大类）标注医疗AI错误，而非仅测量模型是否知道正确答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗AI基准多关注模型是否给出正确答案，而忽视了安全边界失效的具体类型和严重程度，需要一种能系统识别和分类医疗AI安全失效的基准。

Method: 构建临床医生审核的合成病例集（44例），建立安全门分类法（漏报紧急升级、不安全远程给药、不安全出院安抚、证据捏造、不安全协议执行、来源支持缺失）和临床严重度评分标准（1-5级），开发自动化流水线归档模型响应筛选运行，发布HuggingFace排行榜预览。

Result: 发布了v0.2.1版本，包含44个临床医生审核的合成病例及严重程度标注、实时排行榜预览、安全门分类法、严重度评分标准、自动化归档流水线，采用Apache-2.0和CC-BY-4.0许可，无患者数据、临床验证声明或模型排名。

Conclusion: MedFailBench填补了医疗AI安全评估的空白，提供了系统化的错误分类和严重度评估框架，为医疗AI安全研究提供了标准化基准工具。

Abstract: Most medical AI benchmarks measure whether a model knows the correct answer. MedFailBench asks a different question: which safety boundary failed? We present a clinician-built synthetic benchmark and failure atlas that labels medical AI errors by severity (1--5) and safety gate type (missed urgent escalation, unsafe remote dosing, unsafe discharge reassurance, evidence fabrication, unsafe protocol execution, source support gap). The current public release (v0.2.1) contains 44 clinician-reviewed synthetic cases with severity annotations, a live HuggingFace leaderboard preview, a safety gate taxonomy, a clinical severity rubric, and an automated pipeline for archiving model-response screening runs. No patient data, clinical validation claims, or model rankings are included. MedFailBench is released under Apache-2.0 and CC-BY-4.0 and carries the Zenodo DOI 10.5281/zenodo.21205535.

</details>


### [210] [Benchmarking Multimodal Large Language Models for Scientific Visualization Literacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15176)
*Patrick Phuoc Do,Chau M. Ta,Chaoli Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文在标准化的科学可视化素养测试上评测了6个多模态大语言模型，发现Gemini整体表现最强，超越人类平均水平，但开源模型仍低于人类基线；模型在科学插图、搜索和空间理解任务上表现较好，但在纹理和集成可视化及定量估计上表现差；错误分析揭示了细粒度定量估计、流向解读和基于编码解读的反复失败，指出SciVis素养应作为评估多模态AI系统的必要基准维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）评测多以图表为中心，缺乏对科学可视化（SciVis）理解的系统性证据，因此需要建立标准化的SciVis素养基准来全面评估模型在科学可视化任务上的能力。

Method: 采用封闭世界协议，在包含49道题目、18个科学可视化、8种技术和11种任务类型的标准化SciVis素养评估测试上，评测3个闭源和3个开源MLLMs，并与485名人类参与者的表现进行对比。

Result: 结果显示MLLMs不具备统一的SciVis素养：Gemini整体最强，超越人类平均水平；开源模型低于人类基线。性能在技术和任务间高度不均：科学插图、搜索、空间理解表现最好；纹理型和集成型可视化及定量估计表现较差。错误分析揭示细粒度定量估计、流向解读、基于编码解读的反复失败。

Conclusion: 研究确立了SciVis素养作为评估多模态AI系统的必要基准维度，代码和模型输出已公开。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly used to interpret visualizations, yet current evaluations remain largely chart-centric and provide limited evidence of understanding of scientific visualization (SciVis). We benchmark six MLLMs on the scientific visualization literacy assessment test, a standardized SciVis literacy assessment comprising 49 items based on 18 scientific visualizations and illustrations, spanning 8 techniques and 11 task types. We evaluate three closed-source and three open-source models under a closed-world protocol and compare their performance using data from 485 human participants. Results show that current MLLMs do not exhibit uniform SciVis literacy. Gemini is the strongest model overall, exceeding the human mean across the evaluated subsets, whereas the open-source models remain below the human baseline. Performance is highly uneven across techniques and tasks: models perform best on scientific illustration, search, and spatial understanding, but struggle on texture-based and integration-based visualizations and on quantitative estimation. Error analysis reveals recurring failures in fine-grained quantitative estimation, flow-direction interpretation, and grounded encoding interpretation. These findings position SciVis literacy as a necessary benchmark dimension for evaluating multimodal AI systems. Our code and model outputs are publicly available at https://github.com/patdmp/mllm-scivis-lit-benchmark.

</details>


### [211] [Can We Trust Item Response Theory for AI Evaluation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15190)
*Han Jiang,Sunbeom Kwon,Jinwen Luo,Ziang Xiao,Susu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了项目反应理论（IRT）在大语言模型基准测评中的适用性与可靠性。通过模拟六大主流LLM基准的数据，对比四种估计工具在18000种条件下的表现，发现经典估计器在大规模基准下计算不可行，而可扩展估计器在模型数量少或能力分布非正态时会产生不可靠的推断。研究为AI基准测评中使用潜变量模型提供了样本量要求与诊断指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI基准测评越来越多地采用IRT等项目级统计模型来估计模型能力、排名系统、选择信息量大的样本和诊断基准质量。然而，AI基准数据与IRT原本设计的人类测验数据存在显著差异：评测模型数量少、项目数量多、能力分布可能偏态、聚类或多模态。这种制度不匹配可能挑战IRT建模的可靠性，因此需要系统评估IRT在AI评测中的适用边界。

Method: 从六个广泛使用的LLM基准中提取项目参数和能力分布，模拟三种常见IRT模型下的响应矩阵，对比四种估计工具：边际最大似然估计（MML）、马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）、变分推断和神经伪西amese估计器。在18000种模拟条件下，系统评估计算可行性、可扩展性、模型排名、预测性能和项目特征推断的可靠性。

Result: 经典估计器（MML、MCMC）在大规模基准设置下变得计算不可行；可扩展估计器（变分推断、神经伪西amese估计器）在模型数量少或能力分布非正态时，会产生不可靠的项目级和排名推断。研究明确了潜变量模型在何种条件下可靠支持或可能扭曲AI基准测评结论，并给出了可信使用所需的样本量和诊断指标。

Conclusion: IRT在AI基准测评中的应用需谨慎：大规模基准下需采用可扩展估计器，但必须警惕小样本或非正态能力分布带来的推断偏差。建议在使用前进行模拟诊断，确保样本量充足且分布符合假设，才能得出可信的模型排名和项目特征结论。

Abstract: AI benchmarks increasingly leverage item-level statistical models, particularly item response theory (IRT), to estimate model capabilities, rank systems, select informative examples, and diagnose benchmark quality. However, AI benchmark data often departs from the data regime of human testing, for which standard IRT estimation tools were originally developed: benchmarks typically involve fewer evaluated models, far more items, and capability distributions that may be skewed, clustered, or multimodal. We examine how these regime mismatches challenge the reliability of IRT modeling for AI evaluation. Using item parameters and capability distributions derived from six widely used LLM benchmarks, we simulate response matrices under three common IRT models and compare four estimation tools used in recent benchmark studies: marginal maximum likelihood, Markov chain Monte Carlo, variational inference, and a neural pseudo-Siamese estimator. Across 18,000 simulation conditions, we systematically evaluate computational feasibility, scalability, and the reliability of IRT inferences about model rankings, predicted performance, and item characteristics. Results show that classical estimators can become infeasible in large benchmark settings, whereas scalable estimators can produce unreliable item-level and ranking inferences with small or nonnormally distributed model sets. This study identifies when latent trait models reliably support or risk distorting AI benchmarking claims, and what sample sizes and diagnostics are needed for trustworthy use.

</details>


### [212] [Plover: Steering GUI Agents through Plan-Centric Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15193)
*Madhumitha Venkatesan,Shicheng Wen,Jiajing Guo,Jorge Piazentin Ono,Liu Ren,Dongyu Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Plover 是一种以计划为中心的视觉 GUI 自动化系统，将任务计划和重规划外化为可持久化、可检查、可修订的工件，通过规划器-执行器架构支持显式监督、局部修正、自然语言引导和截图定位干预，实验表明可见的计划和局部干预能修复许多自主 GUI 代理的失败，提升透明度、可控性和适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实环境中的 GUI 自动化面临动态布局、意外弹窗、界面状态演变导致自主代理偏离用户意图的问题；现有基于视觉的多模态代理虽提升灵活性，但规划与适应过程内化，限制了用户对系统行为的检查、监督和纠正能力。

Method: 提出 Plover 系统，采用规划器-执行器架构，将任务计划和重规划外化为持久、可检查、可修订的工件；支持显式监督执行演进、通过可编辑计划进行局部修正、自然语言引导、截图定位干预，并在修复过程中保留既有进度；通过 6 名参与者的形成性研究指导交互设计。

Result: 基准失败案例修复和场景化工作流分析表明：当计划保持可见且干预被局部化时，许多自主 GUI 代理的失败在结构上是可修复的；显式重规划使 GUI 自动化更透明、可控、适应性更强。

Conclusion: Plover 验证了将计划外化并支持局部干预的设计能有效修复 GUI 代理常见失败，显著提升自动化的透明度、可控性和适应性，为人机协作的 GUI 自动化提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Graphical user interface (GUI) automation remains challenging in real-world environments, where dynamic layouts, unexpected dialogs, and evolving interface states can cause autonomous agents to drift from user intent. Recent vision-based multimodal agents improve flexibility by operating directly over screenshots and natural language instructions, but planning and adaptation often remain internal, limiting users' ability to inspect, supervise, or correct system behavior. We present Plover, a plan-centric vision-based GUI automation system that externalizes task plans and replanning as persistent, inspectable, and revisable artifacts. Through a planner--executor architecture, Plover supports explicit supervision of evolving execution, localized correction through editable plans, natural-language guidance, and screenshot-grounded interventions, while preserving prior progress during repair. A formative study with six participants informed the interaction design. We then evaluate Plover through benchmark failure-case repair and scenario-based workflow analyses. Our results show that many autonomous GUI-agent failures are structurally repairable when plans remain visible and interventions are localized, and that explicit replanning helps make GUI automation more transparent, controllable, and adaptable.

</details>


### [213] [Self-Evolving Human-Centered Framework for Explainable Depression Symptom Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15202)
*Hoang-Loc Cao,Van Pham,Truong Thanh Hung Nguyen,Phuc Truong Loc Nguyen,Phuc Ho,Veronica Whitford,Hung Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自进化的专家介入标注框架，结合大语言模型辅助标注与专家验证，用于构建符合DSM-5-TR标准的可解释重度抑郁症数据集，包含三阶段流程和双记忆架构，实验表明其提升了标注一致性和可解释性，减少了人工修订工作量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前抑郁症相关数据集标注缺乏结构化证据、症状级理由及与DSM-5-TR标准的可追溯对齐，导致透明度和下游模型可解释性受限，成为构建可靠可解释AI系统的主要瓶颈。

Method: 提出自进化专家介入标注框架，分三阶段：候选证据选择、标准级DSM-5-TR分析、病例级综合生成诊断和严重程度标注；设计双记忆架构（示例记忆和反思记忆）内化专家反馈迭代改进标注，无需重新训练；输出临床证据、推理轨迹和编辑历史以支持可审计性。

Result: 在专家审核样本的初步研究中，该方法提升了标注一致性和可解释性，同时减少了人工修订工作量；多轮反馈循环的评估留待未来工作。

Conclusion: 该框架为构建可解释、符合DSM-5-TR标准的抑郁症数据集提供了可行方案，通过LLM辅助与专家验证结合及记忆机制实现自我进化，有望推动心理健康研究中可靠XAI系统的发展。

Abstract: Annotation quality is a major bottleneck in building reliable and explainable artificial intelligence (XAI) systems for mental health research. In depression-related datasets, labels are often assigned without structured evidence, symptom-level justification, or traceable alignment with the criteria of the Diagnostic and Statistical Manual of Mental Disorders, Fifth Edition, Text Revision (DSM-5-TR), limiting both transparency and downstream model interpretability. We propose a self-evolving, expert-in-the-loop annotation framework for Major Depressive Disorder (MDD) that combines large language model (LLM)-assisted labeling with expert verification. The framework is intended to support the construction of explainable, DSM-5-TR-aligned datasets rather than to perform clinical diagnosis. It operates in three stages: candidate evidence selection from textual records, criterion-level DSM-5-TR analysis, and case-level synthesis that produces label-level diagnostic and severity annotations. A dual-memory architecture, composed of Example Memory and Reflection Memory, is designed to internalize expert feedback and iteratively improve future annotations without retraining. We describe this mechanism and leave its evaluation across multiple feedback cycles to future work. In addition to final labels, the framework exports clinical evidence, reasoning traces, and edit histories, enabling comprehensive auditability. In a pilot study using expert-reviewed samples, the proposed approach improves annotation consistency and explainability while reducing manual revision effort.

</details>


### [214] [When Words Are Safe But Actions Kill: Probing Physical Danger Beyond Text Safety in Hidden-State Risk Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15218)
*Weimeng Wang,Ziqiang Wang,Zihang Zhan,Chuanpu Fu,Qi Li,Ke Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM作为具身智能体规划器时，语言无害指令落地物理世界可能引发的安全风险，证明内容危险(CD)与物理危险(PD)在LLM隐藏状态中可分离，提出单层L2正则化逻辑回归探针PRISM，在多个物理安全基准上显著优于LLM裁判，实现低误报的物理安全检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地作为具身智能体的高层规划器，但语言上良性的指令一旦落地物理世界可能变得不安全。现有文本层面的安全审核无法捕捉这种物理落地危险，需区分内容危险与物理危险，并开发能检测物理落地风险而非仅依赖显性有害关键词的方法。

Method: 通过隐藏状态方向分析和随机分割零测试，在Qwen2.5-3B/7B/14B/32B、Phi-3.5和SmolLM2等模型上验证CD与PD在表征层面可分离。基于此提出PRISM：一种单层L2正则化逻辑回归探针，直接在完整隐藏状态上训练，用于物理安全分类。

Result: PRISM在SafeAgentBench上达到86.2-87.7%准确率，FPR仅11.7-13.7%，远低于同规模LLM裁判的24.7-39.0% FPR。在对比基准PSB-1K上达99.6%准确率，FPR 0.7%，而Qwen2.5-3B裁判拒绝67.8%安全任务。PRISM在SafeText和EARBench上也复现有效性。

Conclusion: 内容危险与物理危险在LLM表征中形成可分离信号，隐藏状态探针是一种超越文本审核的表征级物理安全检测方法。PRISM展示了高效、低误报的物理安全防护能力，为具身智能体的安全规划提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly serve as high-level planners for embodied agents, where linguistically benign instructions can become unsafe once grounded in the physical world. We study whether this physically grounded danger is the same safety problem as ordinary text-level content danger. Through hidden-state direction analysis and random-split null tests, we show that content danger (CD) and physical danger (PD) form separable signals in LLM representations across Qwen2.5-3B/7B/14B/32B, Phi-3.5 and SmolLM2. Building on the CD/PD separability, we propose PRISM, a single-layer L2-regularized logistic probe over full hidden states. PRISM achieves 86.2--87.7\% accuracy on SafeAgentBench with 11.7--13.7\% FPR, while same-scale LLM judges over-block safe tasks at 24.7--39.0\% FPR. We further introduce PhysicalSafetyBench-1K (PSB-1K), a contrastive benchmark of 1{,}000 physical-risk pairs without direct harm keywords, to test whether methods detect physically grounded danger rather than explicit unsafe wording. On PSB-1K, PRISM reaches 99.6\% accuracy and 0.7\% FPR, whereas a Qwen2.5-3B judge rejects 67.8\% of safe tasks. PRISM also replicates on SafeText and EARBench, supporting hidden-state probing as a representation-level method for physical safety beyond text moderation.

</details>


### [215] [AutoSynthesis: An agentic system for automated meta-analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15247)
*Moein Taherinezhad,Sebastian Maier,Gerardo Vitagliano,Francesco Pierri,Stefan Feuerriegel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoSynthesis是一个端到端的多智能体系统，可自动完成从文献检索到荟萃分析的全流程，实验表明其结果与专家手动荟萃分析高度一致，能显著提升证据综合的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定量证据综合对于将初级研究转化为可靠知识至关重要，但目前主要依赖人工操作，难以规模化。需要一种自动化的荟萃分析系统来提高效率和可扩展性。

Method: 提出AutoSynthesis，一个端到端的多智能体系统。给定自然语言研究问题，系统自动制定检索策略、检索文献、筛选研究、评估全文资格、提取定量统计、计算标准化效应量、执行随机效应荟萃分析，并支持异质性分析和偏倚风险评估，最终输出符合PRISMA指南的透明报告。

Result: 在应用中，AutoSynthesis筛选了28项研究并提取了20多个定量结论。其合并效应量估计与专家手动荟萃分析的Hedges' g高度一致，表明自动化结果与人工证据综合具有高度吻合度。

Conclusion: AutoSynthesis能够使定量证据综合更具可扩展性，从而支持跨学科的循证决策。

Abstract: Evidence synthesis is crucial for turning primary research into reliable knowledge for science, medicine, education, and policy. Yet, quantitative evidence synthesis remains largely manual and difficult to scale. Here, we introduce AutoSynthesis, an end-to-end multi-agent system for automated meta-analysis. Given a research question in natural language, AutoSynthesis formulates a search strategy, retrieves scientific literature, screens candidate studies, assesses full-text eligibility, extracts quantitative statistics, computes standardized effect sizes, and finally performs random-effects meta-analysis. AutoSynthesis further supports heterogeneity analysis to examine how effect sizes vary across moderators, as well as risk-of-bias assessment. As output, AutoSynthesis produces a transparent report aligned with PRISMA guidelines. In our application, AutoSynthesis screened over 28 studies and extracted more than 20 quantitative claims. The pooled effect estimates produced by AutoSynthesis are similar to Hedges' $g$ of expert-conducted meta-analyses, indicating close agreement with manual evidence synthesis. Together, these results show that AutoSynthesis can make quantitative evidence synthesis more scalable, thereby supporting evidence-based decision-making across disciplines.

</details>


### [216] [teLLMe Why (Ain't Nothing but a Jam): Exploratory Causal Analysis of Urban Driving Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15254)
*Qiwei Li,Jorge Ortiz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出teLLMe系统，用于城市驾驶数据集的探索性因果分析，结合因果结构学习、Bootstrap稳定性检查、线性回归和DoWhy效应估计，将自然语言问题映射为结构化因果查询，输出“因果卡片”辅助假设生成和专家推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通机构拥有大量视频衍生数据，但多为观测性数据，缺乏干预，难以回答因果问题（如“雨天如何改变交通密度？”），需要工具支持探索性因果分析。

Method: 系统从行车记录仪标注构建结构化事件表，采用PC算法进行因果结构学习，Bootstrap稳定性检查，基于线性回归和DoWhy的查询特定效应估计；利用模式感知LLM将自然语言问题映射为结构化因果查询（指定处理、结果、子人群）；输出“因果卡片”汇总效应估计、调整集、DAG支持、假设及自然语言解释。

Result: 在BDD衍生的交通事件案例研究中，系统能揭示涉及天气、高峰时段和交通密度的合理关系，同时显式展示不确定性和建模选择。

Conclusion: teLLMe设计为假设生成和专家推理工具，而非提供确定性因果结论，旨在使因果分析更透明、可解释，辅助交通安全和拥堵研究。

Abstract: Traffic agencies now have access to large volumes of video-derived data for studying safety and congestion. Most of these data are observational and collected without interventions, which makes causal questions such as "How would rain change traffic density?" difficult to answer. We present teLLMe, a system for exploratory causal analysis of urban driving datasets. The system starts from a structured event table built from dashcam annotations and combines causal structure learning with the PC algorithm, bootstrap-based stability checks, and query-specific effect estimation using linear regression and DoWhy. Natural-language questions are mapped to structured causal queries through a schema-aware LLM, enabling users to specify treatments, outcomes, and subpopulations. teLLMe returns a "Causal Card" that summarizes effect estimates, adjustment sets, DAG support, and assumptions, followed by a short natural-language explanation. Case studies on BDD-derived traffic events show that the system can surface plausible relationships involving weather, peak hours, and traffic density, while making uncertainty and modeling choices explicit. The system is designed as a tool for hypothesis generation and expert reasoning rather than a source of definitive causal claims.

</details>


### [217] [Pretraining Data Can Be Poisoned through Computational Propaganda](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15267)
*Victoria Graf,Hannaneh Hajishirzi,Noah A. Smith,David Kohlbrenner,Kyle Lo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究通过公开讨论接口大规模投毒预训练数据的可行性，提出HalfLife方法评估恶意内容在网络爬取和数据清洗后被包含的程度，证明第三方网页内容可成为攻击语言模型预训练的向量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预训练数据投毒研究主要针对维基百科等固定数据源，未能代表大规模异构预训练语料库，且忽略了投毒数据与数据清洗管道的交互作用。

Method: 利用现有的网络规模内容注入机制——公开讨论接口实施投毒攻击；提出HalfLife分析框架，用于估算对抗性内容在网络爬取和数据清洗后被纳入语言模型训练数据的比例。

Result: 实验表明通过开放讨论接口大规模投毒预训练语料库是可行的；HalfLife分析揭示了估算投毒内容是否被包含在预训练数据中的重要性，确立了第三方网页内容作为攻击语言模型预训练的潜在向量。

Conclusion: 预训练数据投毒攻击在Web规模下通过开放讨论接口是可行的，且数据清洗管道无法完全过滤恶意注入，需建立如HalfLife等评估机制来量化风险。

Abstract: Poisoning pretraining data can introduce harmful behaviors to LMs that are difficult to detect and mitigate. Prior work on poisoning pretraining data has largely exploited established data sources such as Wikipedia, which do not represent the large scale and heterogeneity typical of pretraining corpora, and has ignored the interaction between poisoned data and data curation pipelines. We demonstrate that poisoning attacks on pretraining data are feasible beyond this limited setting through an existing web-scale content injection mechanism: public discussion interfaces. Additionally, to measure whether malicious content is included after web crawling and data curation, we introduce HalfLife, a novel analysis for estimating adversarial content inclusion in web-crawl based LM training data. We use HalfLife to explore the feasibility of poisoning pretraining corpora at web scale through open discussion interfaces. Our analysis demonstrates the importance of estimating whether poison injections are included in pretraining data, and establishes third-party webpage content as a possible vector for attacking language model pretraining.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [218] [Deep-learning Causal Retrieval Optimization for Efficient e-commerce Distribution in Pinterest](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14161)
*Junpeng Hou,XianXing Zhang,Sai Xiao,Derek Cheng,Darren Reger,Olafur Gudmundsson,Mehdi Ben Ayed,Zhiqing Rao,Huizhong Duan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Pinterest构建了一个基于因果推断的生产级系统，用于在早期检索阶段决定何时触发购物候选生成器。通过深度多任务模型结合双重稳健伪结果学习，实现了个性化、上下文感知的触发策略，将购物触发减少高达85%，同时保持关键购物会话不变，并显著提升总会话(+0.26%)和Pin收藏(+1.10%)，同时节省基础设施成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pinterest需要在用户浏览灵感并转化为行动的过程中，精准分发商业内容——在有帮助时展示，在分散注意力时抑制。这被形式化为早期检索阶段触发购物候选生成器的因果决策问题，旨在平衡用户意图、探索成本与基础设施开销。

Method: 采用深度多任务模型联合预测多事件结果与提升，结合双重稳健伪结果与校准结果损失实现稳定的单重稳健提升学习；利用随机化数据记录提供反事实覆盖；设计线性时间离线回放进行阈值选择与策略影响预测；生产部署时模型与远程检索并行运行，避免端到端延迟回归。

Result: 在Web规模部署中，购物触发减少高达85%，关键购物会话保持中性，总会话提升+0.26%，Pin收藏提升+1.10%，并获得显著基础设施节省。离线回放与在线结果表现出极高一致性。

Conclusion: 该工作统一了深度因果学习、可靠的离线回放与生产级部署，为现代级联推荐系统中早期检索优化（超越购物场景）提供了一个通用的实用范式，实现了大规模下探索成本与用户意图的对齐。

Abstract: Pinterest is where people turn inspiration into action as users browse ideas, then take steps toward realization, often by discovering shoppable content. To support this journey, we must distribute commerce content when it helps, not when it distracts. We frame this as a causal decision of triggering shopping candidate generators in early retrieval and deploy a production system at Pinterest that learns personalized and contextualized triggering policies. A deep multi-task model jointly predicts outcomes and uplift of multiple events, trained with a doubly-robust pseudo-outcome alongside calibrated outcome losses for stable, single-robust uplift learning. A randomized data logging supplies counterfactual coverage, and the model is evaluated by both regular and reverse metrics for full assessment. A linear-time offline replay is designed to select thresholds and forecast policy impact with extremely high consistency with online results. For productionization, the model runs in parallel with remote retrieval calls without end-to-end latency regression. At web scale, we cut shopping triggers by up to 85% while holding key shopping sessions neutral, improving important total sessions (+0.26%) and Pin saves (+1.10%), with significant infrastructure savings. By unifying deep causal learning with reliable offline replay and demonstrating production-grade deployment, this work provides a generally practical recipe for early-retrieval optimizations in modern cascading recommenders beyond shopping, aligning exploration and cost with user intent at scale.

</details>


### [219] [Long-History User Transformers for Real-Time Ad Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14331)
*Viacheslav Ovchinnikov,Georgii Smirnov,Nikolai Savushkin,Veronika Ivanova,Maksim Kuzin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种两阶段架构，通过离线Transformer编码用户长序列交互历史为紧凑表示并缓存，在线轻量级模型结合缓存表示、近期事件和请求上下文实时预测CTR，解决了长历史编码与低延迟服务的矛盾，在生产环境中显著提升排名指标和收入且不增加延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线广告中，用户长交互历史对CTR预测极具价值，但实时推理延迟限制（几百毫秒）禁止在请求时运行大型序列编码器，需解决长历史建模与低延迟服务的冲突。

Method: 采用离线-在线解耦两阶段架构：离线高容量Transformer异步编码用户全量跨界面交互历史为紧凑向量缓存至特征存储；在线轻量级模型融合缓存表示、近期事件及请求上下文实时打分。离线编码器在大规模交互日志上自回归预训练，采用双目标（反馈预测+下一项预测），再针对目标广告场景微调CTR预测。

Result: 离线实验显示分离设计恢复了全历史实时Transformer 72%-80% 的质量，缓存表示对陈旧性鲁棒性强，支持低成本刷新策略。生产A/B实验中，搜索广告主排名指标提升+2.77%，Yandex广告网络提升+2.1%，收入分别增长+2.26%和+0.43%，且未增加服务延迟。

Conclusion: 该两阶段架构有效解决了长序列建模与实时低延迟服务的矛盾，通过离线预训练和在线轻量融合，在保持极低延迟的前提下显著提升CTR预测效果和广告收入，已成功部署于大规模生产广告系统。

Abstract: Long interaction histories are among the most informative inputs for click-through rate (CTR) prediction, yet in online advertising they collide with a hard serving constraint: ads must be scored within a few hundred milliseconds to enter the auction, which rules out running a large sequence encoder at request time. We describe how a production advertising system resolves this conflict by decoupling history encoding from real-time inference. A high-capacity offline transformer asynchronously encodes the user's full cross-surface interaction history into a compact representation cached in a feature store, while a lightweight runtime model combines this cached representation with the user's most recent events and the request context at serving time. The offline encoder is pre-trained autoregressively on large-scale interaction logs with a dual objective - feedback prediction and next-item prediction - and the two-stage architecture is then fine-tuned for CTR prediction on the target advertising surface. Offline, the split design recovers 72-80% of the quality of a full-history runtime transformer that would be too expensive to deploy, and the cached representation is robust enough to staleness to permit inexpensive refresh policies. In production A/B experiments, the system improves the primary ranking metric by +2.77% in search advertising and +2.1% on the Yandex Advertising Network, with revenue gains of +2.26% and +0.43% respectively - without increasing serving latency.

</details>


### [220] [LLM-Based Re-Ranking for Real Estate Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14835)
*Nkateko Ntimane,Rafel Guedes,Tiago Cunha,Pedro Nogueira*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: QuintoAndar提出基于LLM的重排序器，用于对话式房地产推荐，利用对话上下文理解用户多维意图，离线和在线实验均显著提升点击率和预约看房率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统房产搜索依赖结构化筛选，难以捕捉用户在多轮对话中表达的复杂、上下文相关的意图；随着对话式助手普及，用户期望通过自然语言表达需求，需要更智能的推荐系统理解隐性偏好。

Method: 构建基于LLM的重排序模型，对检索候选房源进行重排，融合对话历史上下文理解细微意图；构建包含96万查询-条目对的大规模离线评估数据集，采用LLM-as-a-Judge配合人工验证标注；离线评估和在线A/B测试双重验证。

Result: 离线数据集上排名质量提升；在线A/B测试中点击率（CTR）显著提升5.3%，预约看房率提升4.8%。

Conclusion: 将对话上下文整合到房产推荐中，通过LLM重排序有效捕捉用户多维意图，显著提升推荐效果和业务指标，验证了对话式推荐在房地产领域的价值。

Abstract: QuintoAndar Group operates the leading housing marketplace in Latin America for both rentals and sales. The platform replaces traditionally paper-heavy workflows with a fully digital experience, making housing transactions faster and more accessible to tenants, buyers, and landlords in the region. Finding the ideal home in such a vast catalog is inherently difficult. At the same time, the widespread adoption of conversational assistants is reshaping user expectations: people increasingly want to express their needs through open, multi-turn dialog rather than rigid filter menus and faceted search. This shift is particularly pronounced in housing, where intent is multi-dimensional, context-dependent, and rarely reducible to a small set of structured constraints. To meet these expectations, we propose a Large Language Model (LLM) based re-ranker that augments a conversational recommendation system by reordering retrieved candidates according to the nuanced, context-rich intent expressed across the user's conversation. We additionally construct a large-scale offline evaluation dataset for conversational real-estate search, containing 960,000 query-item pairs constructed from both synthetic and production queries and annotated using an LLM-as-a-Judge framework with human validation. We validate our approach both offline, on this proprietary dataset, and online, through a production A/B test. Both evaluations show consistent improvements in ranking quality, including a statistically significant increase in production of +5.3% in click-through rate and +4.8% in scheduled visits, demonstrating the value of integrating conversational context into housing recommendations.

</details>


### [221] [Does generative AI supersede supervised XMLC? A Benchmark Study on Automated Subject Indexing with German Scientific Literature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14882)
*Maximilian Kähler,Katja Konermann,Lisa Kluge,Markus Schumacher*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文将自动主题标引视为极端多标签分类任务，在德国国家图书馆的德语科学文献上对比了监督XMLC方法、词法匹配基线和基于LLM的生成方法。结果显示，基于Transformer密集特征的监督XMLC算法在整体二元相关性指标上表现最佳，而基于LLM的生成方法在分级相关性和长尾词汇表现上更优，是未来生产环境的有前景替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图书馆自动主题标引面临大规模受控词汇表的挑战，属于极端多标签分类问题。需要评估不同方法在真实馆藏数据上的效果，特别是对长尾主题词的推荐能力，以指导实际部署。

Method: 选取多种专门的监督XMLC方法，结合经典词法匹配基线和三个自研的基于LLM的生成方法，在德国国家图书馆收集的当代德语科学文献上进行基准测试。评估指标包括与已有标引的二元相关性对比，以及专业学科馆员的分级相关性打分。

Result: 基于Transformer密集特征的监督XMLC算法在整体二元相关性指标上表现最佳；但关注分级相关性和长尾词汇表现时，基于LLM的生成方法效果更好。所有方法在可靠推荐长尾主题词方面均面临挑战。

Conclusion: 监督XMLC方法适合整体二元相关性优化，而基于LLM的生成方法在分级相关性和长尾覆盖上更具优势，为未来图书馆自动主题标引的生产环境部署提供了有前景的替代方案。

Abstract: With a large controlled vocabulary as the label set, the task of automated subject indexing in a library can be understood as a multi-label classification task. If the set of subject terms is large, the problem fits the Extreme Multi-Label Classification (XMLC) objective. In this study, we apply a selection of specialised supervised XMLC methods to the test case of subject indexing contemporary German scientific literature, collected at the German National Library (DNB). We contrast these results by including a classical lexical matching baseline and three of our own recently developed LLM-based methods into the benchmark. Algorithms are evaluated and compared in several metrics. This includes binary relevance comparisons with previously indexed material, as well as graded relevance ratings by professional subject librarians. A challenge for all methods is to reliably make suggestions from the long tail of the subject vocabulary. We find that supervised XMLC algorithms relying on transformer-based dense features give best results in terms of overall binary relevance metrics. However, focusing on graded relevance and performance in the long tail of our subject vocabulary, the LLM-based generative methods give better results, making them a promising alternative for future productive use.

</details>


### [222] [CoSimRec: Measuring Coordinated-Content Penetration in Recommender Feedback Loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15114)
*Nan Li,Jiahong Shao,Jiuyang Lyu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出 CoSimRec，一种离线基于智能体的评估框架，用于量化推荐系统中协同活动的放大效应，并引入算法渗透率（APR）指标族。实验显示，基于流行度和反馈敏感的排序会显著放大协同内容的曝光与互动，而同步感知排序可有效抑制这种放大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒性评估主要关注静态目标排序变化，无法捕捉协同交互、推荐与用户响应在闭环反馈中的动态演化，因此需要一种能建模协同账号、动态排序、非机器人响应及排序干预的离线评估框架。

Method: 提出 CoSimRec 框架，包含协同账号建模、动态排序、非机器人用户响应模拟及排序干预的闭环流程；设计 APR 指标族，衡量目标内容在非机器人曝光与互动中的份额、相对无攻击基线的提升以及每次协同交互带来的曝光增益。在 MIND、MovieLens、LastFM 三个数据集上，对随机、基于流行度、反馈敏感、MF、BPR-MF 等推荐器进行十种子推理与千用户规模实验。

Result: 随机对照组无统计学显著的正向渗透；基于流行度和反馈敏感的排序在所有六个主从数据集-推荐器设置中均产生显著正向 APR-Lift，LastFM 上最高达 0.4505；同步感知排序在所有对应防御设置中均降低了 APR。

Conclusion: 协同活动在基于流行度和反馈敏感的推荐机制下会被显著放大，导致目标内容获得超额曝光与互动；引入同步感知的排序干预可有效缓解这种放大效应，为推荐系统抗协同操纵提供了可量化的评估与防御依据。

Abstract: Recommender systems increasingly shape which content reaches users, making it important to understand whether coordinated activity is amplified beyond the accounts that initiate it. Existing robustness evaluations largely focus on static target-rank changes and do not capture how coordinated interactions, recommendation, and user response evolve within a feedback loop. To address this gap, we propose CoSimRec, an offline agent-based evaluation framework that models coordinated accounts, dynamic ranking, non-bot responses, and ranking interventions in a shared closed-loop process. CoSimRec introduces the Algorithmic Penetration Rate (APR) metric family to measure target content's share of non-bot exposure and engagement, lift against matched no-attack baselines, and exposure gained per coordinated interaction. We evaluate CoSimRec on MIND, MovieLens, and LastFM using random, popularity-based, feedback-sensitive, MF, and BPR-MF recommenders, with ten-seed inference for the primary APR analysis and population-scale experiments of up to 1000 users. Random controls show no statistically supported positive penetration, whereas popularity-based and feedback-sensitive ranking produce significant positive APR-Lift in all six master-worker dataset--recommender settings, reaching 0.4505 on LastFM; synchronization-aware ranking reduces APR in every corresponding defense setting.

</details>


### [223] [Bridge Evidence: Static Retrieval Utility Does Not Predict Causal Utility in Multi-Step Agentic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15253)
*Debayan Mukhopadhyay,Utshab Kumar Ghosh,Shubham Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究代理式检索中文档的因果效用与静态相关性的差异，提出反事实轨迹效用（CTU）指标，发现约三分之一被代理阅读的文档是“桥梁文档”：静态看无用但因果上关键，它们通过提供判别性实体重定向搜索。静态相关性与因果效用近乎独立，优化前者不保证后者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索系统基于静态效用（SRU）训练评估：单文档配合阅读器回答当前问题。但语言模型作为搜索代理时，会多轮查询推理，文档价值可能在于支持后续动作而非直接回答当前问题，需量化这种差距。

Method: 在 HotpotQA 上用 ReAct 风格代理重放 1000 个开发问题，对每个被读文档执行删除并从该点重跑轨迹，计算反事实轨迹效用（CTU）：最终答案质量、下一查询检索质量、轮数三个维度的增量。将 CTU 与静态 RAG 效用（SRU）在 23,322 个文档观测上对比，并用可观测实体相关性（OER）分析判别性实体在下一查询中的出现频率。

Result: CTU 与 SRU 斯皮尔曼相关系数 -0.026，近乎统计独立。约 33% 代理读取的文档是因果承重但静态无用的桥梁文档；用 BM25 和交叉编码器代理 SRU 后桥梁比例 27.2%。判别性实体在代理下一查询中出现频率是仅现于非相关文档实体的 4.02 倍（6.1% vs 1.5%，n=227,139）。桥梁文档通过提供判别性实体重定向搜索发挥作用。

Conclusion: 代理式检索中静态相关性与因果效用是两个不同量，优化静态相关性无法直接获得因果效用。未来检索训练需显式建模文档对后续轨迹的因果贡献，而非仅关注当前问题的答案改进。

Abstract: Retrieval systems are trained and evaluated on a static idea of usefulness: hand a document and a question to a reader model, see whether the answer improves, and score the document accordingly. The idea holds up when a document is read on its own. It breaks when a language model works as a search agent, issuing several queries and reasoning across turns, because a document can matter for what it lets the agent do next rather than for what it says about the current question.
  We measure that gap rather than argue it. Using a ReAct style agent over HotpotQA, we replay 1000 development questions and, for every document the agent read, delete it and re-run the rest of the trajectory from that point. Comparing the original run against its counterfactual gives a Counterfactual Trajectory Utility (CTU) score from three deltas: final answer quality, next query retrieval quality, and turn count. Crossing CTU against Static RAG Utility (SRU) over 23,322 document observations, the two are close to statistically independent (Spearman rho = -0.026). Roughly a third of the documents the agent reads are causally load bearing while looking useless to a static reader; we call these bridge documents. The pattern survives when the reader based axis is swapped for a BM25 and cross encoder proxy, giving a bridge cell of 27.2% on an evenly spread axis.
  A second experiment pins down the mechanism. Using the Observable Entity Relevance (OER) measure from prior work, entities that discriminate relevant from non-relevant candidates appear in the agent's next query 4.02 times more often than entities found only in non-relevant documents (6.1% vs 1.5%, n = 227,139). A bridge document earns its keep by handing the agent a discriminative entity that redirects the search. Static relevance and causal usefulness are different quantities in agentic retrieval, and optimizing the first does not deliver the second.

</details>
